The `.syntax` extension that scala.meta provides via wildcard is routed through `DomainScalaParseBack.renderS30(tree)` instead. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal (215 Scala goldens / 28 domains × 2 dialects all match), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **`DomainSTContext`, `DomainScalaTranslator`, `ModuleTools.toSource` all unchanged**. **Surface area remaining for F-TextTree** (M7+): renderers — `DomainServiceRenderer` 129 LOC, `DomainServiceMethodProduct` 447 LOC, `DomainServiceContext` 82 LOC. Extensions (M8) — `DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase` 504, `DomainCastSimilarExtension` 162, `DomainCastUpExtension` 273, `DomainCastDownExpandExtension` 127, `DomainAnyvalExtension` 152 — total ~1218 LOC. Once extensions emit `String` directly the carrier can flip String-everywhere and `DomainScalaParseBack` retires, completing scala.meta removal. **Path to M7 (services + service-method-product)**: same parse-back strategy applies; `CogenServiceProduct` carries 6 `Defn` types (server / client / clientWrapped / serverWrapped / methods / marshallers) — wider carrier than M5/M6 but uniform shape. `DomainServiceMethodProduct` (447 LOC) is the heaviest single file; expect 2-3 review rounds for byte parity. The 1500-LOC stop condition stays comfortable.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M5: port Scala enum + ADT renderers off scala.meta** (2026-05-12) — Fifth milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; second cycle on the Scala renderer family. Migrates `DomainEnumRenderer` and `DomainAdtRenderer` from `scala.meta` quasiquotes onto `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]` composition. **Renderers ported**: `DomainEnumRenderer.scala` 69 → 87 LOC; `DomainAdtRenderer.scala` 58 → 111 LOC (growth is renderer-protocol documentation + per-witness substitution sites). **Foundation extended**: `ScalaRefHandle.scala` 52 → 77 LOC — added three witness cases for the new substitution shapes: `TypeAbsolute(typeId)` (mirrors `ScalaType.typeAbsolute` for the `_root_.`-rooted ADT branch target type), `TypeFullWithin(parentId, name)` (qualified type-position reference for a nested name e.g. an ADT branch case class, used by the `from<Branch>` parameter type), `TermFullWithin(parentId, name)` (term-position counterpart for the `into<Branch>` constructor call site). `DomainScalaTextResolver.scala` 51 → 57 LOC — resolver + `harvestTypeIds` updated to dispatch on the new cases. **Boundary helper**: `DomainScalaParseBack.scala` (new, 56 LOC) — lowers a rendered Scala source fragment to a `scala.meta.Defn.{Trait, Object, Class, Def}` via `dialect.parse[Stat]` so the legacy `EnumProduct` / `AdtProduct` carriers (which carry `Defn` and are mutated by Circe / AnyVal / Cast* extensions via `prependBase` / `appendDefinitions`) continue to work unmodified. **Carrier-migration strategy applied** (deviation from M4's recommendation): parse-back-at-boundary chosen over String-everywhere carriers because the existing `EnumProduct` / `AdtProduct` are consumed by ≥4 callers — the per-typeDef emitters in `DomainScalaTranslator` (Circe sibling + base prepend, `more :+ circe.defn`), `DomainServiceMethodProduct.withAdtCirce` (service-output ADT inside service objects), and the existing harness specs (`DomainAdtRendererSpec` consumes `product.render: List[Defn]`). Migrating the carrier would have cascaded into ~5 product-and-extension files (≥1500 LOC blast radius) and is deferred. **Byte-parity pitfalls surfaced** (both fixed inline): (1) `q"trait F extends X {}".syntax` drops the empty braces (scala.meta's printer normalises them out) but `parse("trait F extends X {}").syntax` preserves them — the renderer therefore omits the trailing `{}` from the trait declaration text (`sealed trait $name extends $base` with no braces). (2) `ctx.conv.toScala[Product].init()` renders the minimized `typeFull` shape (`scala.Product`, not `_root_.scala.Product`); the M5 ADT renderer pre-computes `productRef = ctx.conv.toScala(classOf[Product]).typeFull.toString` to mirror this. Both pitfalls visible only at byte-equality verification; structural correctness alone would have passed. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal (215 Scala goldens / 28 domains × 2 dialects all match), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **`DomainScalaTranslator.scala` unchanged**: the parse-back strategy preserves the carrier's `Defn` surface so the translator's per-renderer emit (Circe + AnyVal + Cast* wiring) operates unmodified. **Path to M6 (structural renderers: identifier + DTO + interface)**: (1) `DomainIdRenderer` 125 LOC, `DomainCompositeRenderer` 122 LOC, `DomainInterfaceRenderer` 192 LOC + supporting `DomainScalaStruct` 233 LOC + `DomainCompositeStructure` 193 LOC — same parse-back strategy applies but each renderer's `Defn.Class` output is consumed by more extension hooks (AnyVal `.prependBase`, CastSimilar / CastUp / CastDownExpand `appendDefinitions`, Circe sibling + base). Witness ADT may need `TermFull(typeId)` and `TypeWithMembers` cases for the parse method of identifiers. (2) `DomainServiceRenderer` 129 LOC + `DomainServiceMethodProduct` 447 LOC — the heaviest single file in the renderer family. (3) Extensions (Circe 504 LOC, Cast* 562 LOC, Anyval 152 LOC) migrate last; once they emit `String` directly, the carrier can flip to String-everywhere and `DomainScalaParseBack` retires. The 1500-LOC stop condition is comfortable for any M6+ cycle.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M4: Scala migration foundation (witness + resolver + alias renderer)** (2026-05-12) — Fourth milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite; first cycle to attack the Scala renderer family. Migrates the simplest renderer (alias-into-package-object emission) off `scala.meta` quasiquotes onto `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]`, establishing the witness + resolver pattern Scala-side. **Foundation** (2 new files, 101 LOC): `ScalaRefHandle.scala` (51 LOC) — sealed ADT with two cases: `TypeName(typeId)` (bare LHS-position, mirrors legacy `ScalaType.typeName`) and `TypeFull(typeId)` (qualified RHS-position, mirrors legacy `ScalaType.typeFull`). The dual-case shape preserves the legacy renderer's `.typeName` / `.typeFull` distinction at the witness layer rather than at the resolver call site, keeping the resolver dispatch one-line. `DomainScalaTextResolver.scala` (50 LOC) — delegates to the existing `ScalaTypeConverter` (the single source of truth for `TypeId → Scala name` lowering); `.toString` on the converter's `Type.Name` / `Type.Select` / `Type.Apply` shapes produces the same Scala source the legacy `Defn.toString()` produced for the wrapping `Defn.Type`. Includes `harvestTypeIds` for the future import-collection pass. **Alias renderer ported** (`DomainAliasRenderer.scala`, 30 → 54 LOC, most growth is renderer-protocol documentation): composes `q"type $lhs = $rhs"` as `TextTree[ScalaRefHandle]` and renders to `Seq[String]` at the product boundary. **Product-carrier strategy**: the parse-back-to-`Defn` trick was *not* needed. The legacy alias path never went through `dialect(tree).syntax` (the `ModuleTools.toSource` serialization point) — alias `Defn` instances are joined into the per-package `package object` body via `Defn.toString()` directly inside `DomainScalaTranslator.translate()` (lines 109-124). Changing `aliasEntries: ArrayBuffer[(ModuleId, Seq[Defn])]` → `Seq[String]` and dropping the `_.toString()` step from the body-join yields a fully `scala.meta`-free alias path end-to-end, no parse round-trip. This was a fortunate property of the alias path specifically; the structural / service / extension renderers (M5+) still need the carrier-migration design decision. **Byte parity**: source goldens unchanged — `verifyGoldens` byte-equal on both Scala versions. The relaxed M3 parity convention (locked at IMPL-7a.2 Phase B M3) applies — the real contract is wire-format JSON byte-equality via `runWireFixtures` + `runCrossLangInterop`, which passes; source byte-equality between new and legacy is not a hard gate but happens to hold here because the rendered text shape is bit-identical to `Defn.toString()`. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (byte-equal output, no golden regeneration), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **Surface area assessment for M5+**: ~14 remaining `scala.meta`-bound files under `toscala/domain/` totaling ~3,300 LOC (renderers: `DomainEnumRenderer` 69, `DomainIdRenderer` 125, `DomainAdtRenderer` 58, `DomainCompositeRenderer` 122, `DomainInterfaceRenderer` 192, `DomainServiceRenderer` 129, `DomainServiceMethodProduct` 447; supporting: `DomainScalaStruct` 233, `DomainCompositeStructure` 193, `DomainServiceContext` 82; extensions: `DomainAnyvalExtension` 152, `DomainCastSimilarExtension` 162, `DomainCastUpExtension` 273, `DomainCastDownExpandExtension` 127, `DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase` 504). Plus the product-carrier layer: `CogenProduct` (108 LOC, carrying `Defn` instances of `Trait`/`Class`/`Object`) and `RenderableCogenProduct` (53 LOC), and the central serialization point `ModuleTools.toSource` which calls `dialect(tree).syntax` on each `Defn` (lines 20-21). **Path to M5 (full scala.meta retirement)**: (1) enum + ADT renderers next (simplest non-alias shapes, smaller `Defn` graphs), under the same `Seq[String]` carrier; (2) decide product-carrier migration policy (String-everywhere vs. case-by-case parse-back-to-Defn) before the structural-renderer wave (DTO/Interface/Id) — recommendation is String-everywhere with `CogenProduct.companion: String` etc., mirroring the C# product shape; (3) migrate `ModuleTools.toSource` to plain `(preamble +: parts).mkString("\n\n")` once all products carry String; (4) extensions migrate last (Circe is the heaviest at 504 LOC and exercises every meta shape — DefMatch, Term.Match cases, implicit Encoder/Decoder traits — and will likely surface the most pitfalls). The 1500-LOC stop condition is comfortably within reach for any single M5+ cycle. Witness ADT is sealed-extensible: add `TermName` / `TermFull` cases when the term-position references surface in the service/extension renderers.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M3: port C# renderers under typed protocol** (2026-05-12) — Third milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite. Introduces the C# typed-renderer protocol mirroring TS M1.5/M2. **Foundation**: `CSRefHandle` (new file, 42 LOC) — sealed ADT carrying `TypeRef(typeId: TypeId)`; `DomainCSTypeResolver` (new file, 49 LOC) — resolves `CSRefHandle` to fully-qualified C# identifier via `DomainCSharpType(id).renderType(true)`, plus `harvestTypeIds` for the future option-B' import pass. The C# renderer surface is uniform in that every renderer-level call site is `renderType(true)`; the few `renderType(false)` paths live inside `DomainCSharpType` itself (`renderFromString`, the converter's user-type case) and are not exercised from renderer envelopes, so a single `TypeRef` case is sufficient for byte-parity. **Renderers ported** (7 files, `+228 / -195` net): `DomainCSAliasRenderer` (47 → 48), `DomainCSEnumRenderer` (93 → 91), `DomainCSCompositeRenderer` (66 → 57), `DomainCSIdRenderer` (96 → 96), `DomainCSInterfaceRenderer` (130 → 121), `DomainCSAdtRenderer` (111 → 133), `DomainCSServiceMethodProduct` (267 → 274), `DomainCSServiceRenderer` (284 → 307). Each renderer's envelope is composed as `TextTree[CSRefHandle]` rendered via `.mapRender(resolver.resolve)` at the product boundary (option B''-lite from M1.5). `DomainCSNameMangling` left unchanged (pure name helpers; no type-reference emission). **JsonNetExtension protocol-adoption deferred**: `DomainCSJsonNetExtension` (610 LOC) is a `String`-helper module — its `preDto`/`postStruct`/`postAdt`/`postEnum` etc. emit code fragments spliced inline as `String` by the renderer envelopes. Since the extension's helpers do not return products and its consumers (the renderers) already render via `mapRender`, TextTree adoption inside the extension yields no harvest benefit; the type references in the extension's output are pre-resolved by direct `DomainCSharpType.renderType(true)` calls. Tracking this as a low-priority follow-up — the typed protocol's harvest benefit only surfaces at the envelope-producing layer, not in `String`-helper modules consumed downstream. **TextTree caveat surfaced**: the C# renderer family interpolates `Int` values into headers (`fields.length`, `prefixLength` in `IdRenderer`); the `q"..."` interpolator infers `TextTree[Int]` and fails the `TextTree[CSRefHandle]` constraint. Workaround: bind each Int to a `.toString` `val` before interpolation. Documented inline; identical pattern would surface in any future numeric-payload renderer. **M2 stripMargin pitfall not triggered**: no C# envelope splits a polymorphic type-union (`A | B`) across an interpolation boundary, so the M2 single-StringNode precompose workaround is not needed here. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (byte-equal output, no golden regeneration), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **Path to Scala migration**: tracked separately as **F-Scala-TextTree**; the existing `toscala/domain/` uses `scala.meta.quasiquotes` — a different surface (AST-based, not string-template) that may not benefit from TextTree adoption and is deferred pending separate evaluation.

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M2: port remaining TS renderers under typed protocol** (2026-05-12) — Second milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite. Nine files ported to emit `TextTree[TSRefHandle]` and render via `.mapRender(resolver.resolve)` at the product boundary (option B''-lite, locked at M1.5): `DomainTSEnumRenderer` (43 → 44), `DomainTSIdRenderer` (139 → 145), `DomainTSCompositeRenderer` (176 → 179), `DomainTSInterfaceRenderer` (180 → 187), `DomainTSAdtRenderer` (138 → 147), `DomainTSServiceMethodProduct` (363 → 368), `DomainTSServiceRenderer` (352 → 334), `DomainTSEnumHelpersExtension` (43 → 44), `DomainTSIntrospectionExtension` (292 → 287). Supporting infra: `TSRefHandle` extended with `SerializedTypeRef(typeId)` (39 → 47 LOC); `DomainTSTypeResolver.resolve` + `harvestTypeIds` updated to dispatch on the new case (51 → 55). `DomainTSNameMangling` left unchanged (pure name helpers; no type-reference emission). **TextTree.stripMargin pitfall**: when the `q"..."` interpolator splits a logical text line across an interpolation boundary, a literal `|` adjacent to the interpolation gets isolated in its own `StringNode`, where line-start = `|`, and the per-`StringNode` margin stripper removes it. Surfaced in the Interface companion's polymorphic-section type-union (`data?: $eid| ${eid}Serialized`); fixed by precomposing the substring into a single `String` value (`${eid + "| " + eid + "Serialized"}`) so the `|` is non-line-leading inside one `StringNode`. **Option B' did not materialize**: `DomainTSImports` still owns import computation; lifting `.mapRender` from per-renderer to per-output-file would require threading `TextTree[TSRefHandle]` through every product type and rewriting `DomainTypeScriptTranslator.translate()` — deferred to a future cycle once the cross-language port settles. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (byte-equal output, no golden regeneration needed beyond the interface-renderer transient fix), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **M3 (next cycle)**: `tocsharp/domain/` (~12 files) under a new `CSRefHandle` ADT + `DomainCSTypeResolver`. Path to Scala migration tracked separately as **F-Scala-TextTree** (the existing `toscala/domain/` uses `scala.meta.quasiquotes` — different surface, deferred).

- **PR-02 F-TextTree M1: adopt TextTree in DomainTSAliasRenderer (foundation cycle)** (2026-05-12) — First milestone of the F-TextTree multi-cycle rewrite. Ports `idealingua-v1/idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/totypescript/domain/DomainTSAliasRenderer.scala` from a `stripMargin`-based raw string template to a `TextTree[Nothing]` composition rendered via `.render` at the `AliasProduct.content: String` boundary. Renderer protocol decision: **option (a)** — renderers internally compose `TextTree` and convert to `String` at the product boundary; `Module(path, content: String)` shape unchanged. Option (b) (threading `TextTree` to the layouter) rejected for M1 because it cascades through every product type in `toscala/products/`, `totypescript/products/`, `tocsharp/products/`. Byte-parity preserved by appending the 10-space trailing pad that the legacy `stripMargin` template left in the output (closing `"""` was column-11-aligned in the source). LOC: 52 → 90 (most growth is renderer-protocol documentation). Scope refinement: full F-TextTree rewrite affects ~9,700 LOC across the three renderer trees (previous "~3000 LOC" estimate was low by ~3x). `TextTree` API findings: ADT (`StringNode` / `VerbatimNode` / `ValueNode[T]` / `Node[T]` / `Shift[T]` / `Trim[T]`); constructors `TextTree.text` / `verbatim` / `value`; generic ops `.render` (requires `T =:= Nothing`) / `.mapRender(f)` / `.shift(n)` / `.trim` / `.stripMargin` / `.flatten` / `.map`; seq ops `.join(sep)` / `.joinN()` / `.joinNN()`; `q"..."` and `qv"..."` interpolators via `Quote(StringContext)` implicit. Idiom confirmed against `baboon-compiler` swift translators (`q"""...|${xs.joinN().shift(N).trim}"""` style). **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok, `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **M2..MN remaining**: rest of `totypescript/domain/` (~15 files including `DomainTSEnumRenderer`, `DomainTSIdRenderer`, `DomainTSInterfaceRenderer`, `DomainTSCompositeRenderer`, `DomainTSAdtRenderer`, `DomainTSServiceRenderer`, `DomainTSServiceMethodProduct`, and the two extensions); then `toscala/domain/` (~10 files — these currently use `scala.meta.quasiquotes`, a different surface that may be left as-is); then `tocsharp/domain/` (~12 files). Each renderer ports independently with `verifyGoldens` as the byte-level safety net.

- **PR-02 F5-fix: TBLOB → base64 string on all three emitters (+ corpus fixture)** (2026-05-12) — Closes F5 (TBLOB Q3 divergence) + F2 (TBLOB cross-language verification pre-baseline). All three transpilers now wire-encode TBLOB as a standard-alphabet padded base64 string per Q3 lock.

  **Per-emitter changes**:
  - **Scala** (`idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-scala/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/runtime/circe/IRTTimeInstances.scala` + `idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../toscala/types/ScalaTypeConverter.scala`). TBLOB still maps to `Array[Byte]`; `IRTTimeInstances` now exposes implicit `Encoder[Array[Byte]]` / `Decoder[Array[Byte]]` via `java.util.Base64.getEncoder/getDecoder`. Every generated `XCirce` trait extends `IRTTimeInstances` (via `Init(...IRTTimeInstances.typeAbsolute, ...)` in `DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase`), so the override is in scope for `deriveEncoder`/`deriveDecoder`. `ScalaTypeConverter.toScala(id: TypeId)` adds a `Primitive.TBLOB` case that routes through the private `toScala(JavaType, args)` generic path with `Array[Byte]` element parameterization (the prior `toScala(toPrimitive(TBLOB))` path was dead — it would have thrown `IDLException("No type->generic mappings implemented yet")` on the parameterised `JavaType("Array", Seq("Byte"))`).
  - **TypeScript** (`idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../totypescript/domain/DomainTSTypeConverter.scala` + `…/domain/extensions/DomainTSIntrospectionExtension.scala` + `idealingua-v1-runtime-rpc-typescript/.../irt/introspector.ts`). TBLOB → `string` (base64 is the TS-layer representation). All 7 `???` sites in `DomainTSTypeConverter` collapse to string-passthrough (mirrors `TUUID` shape — pass `value` / `name` / `variable` through unchanged). `toFieldMethods` routes through `toStringField` (no GUID-format validation); `toFieldMember` uses `"string"`. `DomainTSIntrospectionExtension.unwindType` emits `{intro: IntrospectorTypes.Blob}`; `Blob` added as the trailing enum member to `IntrospectorTypes` so existing wire IDs stay byte-stable.
  - **C#** (`idealingua-v1-transpilers/.../tocsharp/domain/DomainCSharpType.scala` + `…/domain/DomainCSImports.scala` + `…/domain/extensions/DomainCSJsonNetExtension.scala`). TBLOB → `byte[]`. `isNullable=true` (reference type). `renderPrimitiveType` returns `"byte[]"`; `getRandomValue` emits a `new byte[] { ... }` literal with 1-8 random bytes; `renderToString`/`renderFromString` use `System.Convert.ToBase64String`/`FromBase64String` (URL-escape-safe at the identifier-string layer). `DomainCSJsonNetExtension.writePropertyValue` emits `writer.WriteValue(System.Convert.ToBase64String($src));`; reader emits `System.Convert.FromBase64String($src.Value<string>())`. `propertyNeedsPrepare=false` (single-value scalar write — no JArray/JObject loop). `DomainCSImports.withImport(TBLOB)` returns `Seq("System")`.

  **Corpus fixture**: new `idealingua-v1-test-defs/src/main/resources/defs/main-tests/source/idltest/blobtest.domain` declares `data BlobHolder { payload: blb; label: str }`. Three wire-fixture files `wire-fixtures/<lang>/idltest.blobtest.BlobHolder/with-bytes.json` each contain `{"payload":"aGkh","label":"greeting"}` — 3-byte UTF-8 `"hi!"` encoded as base64 `aGkh` (no padding required at length 4). `WireDispatch` (Scala), `Dispatch.ts`, `Dispatch.cs` each register the new wireId.

  **Wire-format byte-encoding chosen**: standard base64 alphabet (`A-Z`, `a-z`, `0-9`, `+`, `/`) with padding (`=`). Matches Java `Base64.getEncoder()` defaults, Newtonsoft.Json `byte[]` default, and TS string-passthrough (which simply preserves whatever the Scala or C# side emitted). URL-safe alphabet rejected: not standard for JSON payloads, would require per-language opt-in helpers.

  **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok against regenerated goldens (3 new files: `golden/scala/idltest/blobtest/BlobHolder.scala`, `golden/typescript/idltest/blobtest/BlobHolder.ts` + `index.ts`, `golden/csharp/Idltest/Blobtest/BlobHolder.cs`), `runWireFixtures` 26 Scala + 20 TypeScript + 21 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 118 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded (was 112/10/6 — the new BlobHolder wireId contributes +6 verified entries, one per language pair), `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42.

  **Surprises**:
  - The Scala-leg `ScalaTypeConverter.toScala(JavaType)` public arm throws when `parameters.nonEmpty`; the legacy `toPrimitive(TBLOB)` always produced a parameterised `JavaType` ("Array", Seq("Byte")). Conclusion: legacy code path for TBLOB on the Scala leg was dead — no domain ever exercised it through the new `Domain<Lang>Translator` path. Fix routes TBLOB through the private generic-friendly arm.
  - `IRTTimeInstances.Decoder[Array[Byte]]` overrides the default Circe `Decoder[Array[Byte]]` (which decodes int-arrays). Risk: if any user code outside the generated codecs relied on the default int-array form, the override could shadow it. Mitigation: the override is on the trait, not as a top-level implicit — only code paths that mix in `IRTTimeInstances` (i.e. the generated wire codecs) see the change. End-user code that imports `io.circe.generic.auto.*` directly still gets the default.
  - C# `Driver.dll` rebuild gating: `CSharpDriverBridge.ensureBuild` only runs `dotnet build` if `Driver.dll` does not exist. After adding the new dispatch entry, the C# harness was rerun with the stale binary — surfaced as `UnknownWireId`. Cleared `bin/` + `obj/` to force rebuild; first wire-fixtures run after the clean produced a fresh `Driver.dll` covering the new BlobHolder dispatch entry. The gating is otherwise correct (`dotnet build` is slow on first run) but a follow-up could compare source mtimes against `Driver.dll` mtime to invalidate proactively.

- **PR-02 IMPL-12: collapse `CompilerOptions[E, M]` → `CompilerOptions[M]` (extension type parameter retired)** (2026-05-12) — The `E <: TranslatorExtension` type parameter chain became degenerate post-IMPL-10c: every concrete `<Lang>TranslatorExtension` marker trait was inhabited only by `Seq.empty` in the corresponding `<Lang>DefaultExtensions` object, no renderer iterated `options.extensions`, and the three `Domain*Context.extensions` accessors had zero callers. IMPL-12 retires the parameter end-to-end. **Deleted (10 files)**: `TranslatorExtension.scala` (trait + `ExtensionId`), `toscala/extensions/ScalaTranslatorExtension.scala`, `totypescript/extensions/TypeScriptTranslatorExtension.scala`, `tocsharp/extensions/CSharpTranslatorExtension.scala`, `toscala/domain/ScalaDefaultExtensions.scala`, `totypescript/domain/TypeScriptDefaultExtensions.scala`, `tocsharp/domain/CSharpDefaultExtensions.scala`, plus the three now-empty `extensions/` directories. **Edited**: `CompilerOptions.scala` (`AbstractCompilerOptions[M]`, `CompilerOptions[M]`, `UntypedCompilerOptions` — `extensions` field dropped from each; `CompilerOptions.from[M]` no longer takes `E: ClassTag`); `TranslatorDescriptor.scala` (dropped `def defaultExtensions: Seq[TranslatorExtension]`); `ScalaTranslatorDescriptor` / `TypescriptTranslatorDescriptor` / `CSharpTranslatorDescriptor` (dropped `defaultExtensions` override + `<Lang>DefaultExtensions` import); `TranslationLayouter.scala` (`CompilerOptions[?, ?]` → `CompilerOptions[?]`); `DomainSTContext` / `DomainTSContext` / `DomainCSContext` (dropped the dead `final def extensions = options.extensions` accessors + adjusted docstrings); `CommandlineIDLCompiler.scala` (`getExt` deleted; `toOptions` no longer threads `exts`); `IDLCArgs.scala` (`LanguageOpts.extensions` field deleted; `LP.extensionSpec` parser arg deleted; `extensions = ...` removed from `LanguageOpts` construction); `HarnessOptions.scala` (dropped `extensions = TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.descriptor(lang).defaultExtensions` + the `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade` import); `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec.scala` (dropped `extensions = ...` from three `UntypedCompilerOptions` ctors); 10 harness specs (`DomainIdRendererSpec`, `DomainCompositeRendererSpec`, `DomainAdtRendererSpec`, `DomainInterfaceRendererSpec`, `DomainServiceRendererSpec`, `DomainAnyvalExtensionSpec`, `DomainCastSimilarExtensionSpec`, `DomainCastUpExtensionSpec`, `DomainCastDownExpandExtensionSpec`, `DomainCirceExtensionSpec`) — dropped `import ...ScalaTranslatorExtension`, dropped `private val emptyExts: Seq[ScalaTranslatorExtension] = Seq.empty`, switched `CompilerOptions[ScalaTranslatorExtension, ScalaBuildManifest](IDLLanguage.Scala, emptyExts, X)` → `CompilerOptions[ScalaBuildManifest](IDLLanguage.Scala, X)`. **`--extensions` CLI flag fate (C9 hard-removal)**: `IDLCArgs.LP.extensionSpec` (`-e / --extensions {* | -AnyvalExtension;-CirceDerivationTranslatorExtension}`) deleted along with the field on `LanguageOpts`; the flag never reached any production wiring after IMPL-10c (the filtered seq was empty regardless of spec), so removal is a no-op for any user who previously passed it. **`M` type parameter retained**: manifests are still real per-language types (`ScalaBuildManifest` / `TypeScriptBuildManifest` / `CSharpBuildManifest`). **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (no regeneration needed), `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42.

- **PR-02 IMPL-10d: delete legacy Typespace + IDLTyper + TypespaceImpl + verification subtree** (2026-05-12) — Final atomic deletion of the legacy typer + `Typespace` query machinery + post-typing renderer tree. **Deleted**: `Typespace` trait (`typespace/Typespace.scala`) + `TypespaceImpl` + `StructuralQueriesImpl` + `InheritanceQueriesImpl` + `TypeCollection` + `TypespaceToolsImpl` + `FieldExtractor` (7 files in `typespace/`); entire `typespace/verification/` subtree — `TypespaceVerifier` + `VerificationRule` + `MissingDependency` + 8 rule files (`AdtConflictsRule`, `AdtMembersRule`, `BasicNamingConventionsRule`, `CyclicImportsRule`, `CyclicInheritanceRule`, `CyclicUsageRule`, `DuplicateMemberRule`, `ReservedKeywordRule`); `IDLTyper.scala` (which contained both `IDLTyper` + `IDLPostTyper` + `IDLPretyper`); `il/ast/typed/DomainDefinition.scala` (the legacy typed-domain case class with its `referenced: Map[DomainId, IDLPostTyper]` field — split off the surviving `DomainMetadata` + `Inclusion` types to a new `DomainMetadata.scala` since the new typer still consumes them); entire `il/renderer/` subtree (24 files: `IDLRenderer`, `IDLRenderingContext`, `RDomain`, `RTypeDef`, `RAdtMember`, `RTypeId`, `RIdField`, `RField`, `RAnno`, `RStructure`, `RSimpleStructure`, `RService`, `RBuzzer`, `RStreams`, `RTypedStream`, `RValue`, `MetaRenderer`, `MethodRenderer`, `RDomainId`, `RStreamDirection`, `package.scala`); `LoaderTest.scala` (sole consumer of `IDLRenderer` was the parse→render→parse roundtrip, which is meaningless without the legacy typed domain). **Created**: `il/ast/IDLPretyper.scala` (the Phase-0 raw→loaded projection, extracted verbatim from the deleted `IDLTyper.scala` so `NewTyperPipeline.run` keeps its sole legacy-surviving helper); `il/ast/typed/DomainMetadata.scala` (carries the case classes `Inclusion` + `DomainMetadata` previously in `DomainDefinition.scala`). **Rewritten**: `LoadedDomain.scala` — `Success` no longer carries `typespace: Typespace`; the case class signature is `Success(path: FSPath, parsed: DomainMeshResolved, warnings: Vector[IDLWarning])`. `ModelResolver.scala` — drops the `rules: Seq[VerificationRule]` constructor parameter, drops `runVerifier` / `runTyper`, collapses `makeTyped` into `makeLoaded` that just constructs `Success(parsed.origin, parsed, Vector.empty)`. `DuplicateDomainsRule.scala` — reads `s.parsed.id` instead of `s.typespace.domain.id`. `NegativeSpec.scala` — now invokes `NewTyperPipeline.run(d.parsed)` after `HarnessCorpus.loadCorpus(caseDir)` so the new typer rejects each `.must-reject` corpus fixture (the legacy spec relied on the loader rejecting via the deleted typer / verifier). `IDLError.scala` — drops `MissingDependencies(deps: List[MissingDependency])` case along with the `MissingDependency` import (the new typer never produces this error; structural / cyclic-inheritance diagnostics from `NewTyperPipeline` already cover the same surface). `TranslatorDescriptor.scala` + the three concrete `<Lang>TranslatorDescriptor.scala` — drop the `def rules: Seq[VerificationRule]` member + `ReservedKeywordRule.warning(...)` call (legacy keyword-warning rules ran inside `TypespaceVerifier` which is gone; the new-typer pipeline carries its own `BasicNamingConventionsRule`). Consumer-side updates: `CommandlineIDLCompiler.runCompiler` drops the `val rules = ...` line and instantiates `new ModelResolver()`; `HarnessCorpus.loadCorpus` drops `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade` import + `rules` plumbing; `IDLTestTools.makeResolver` drops the per-language rules lookup + the `scala.util.Try` import; `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec.loadEnumsDomain` drops `rules` + replaces `.typespace.domain.id` access with `.parsed.id`; `TyperDiagnosticsSpec` swaps `domain.typespace.domain.id.toString` → `domain.parsed.id.toString`. **RETAINED**: `typespace/structures/` subtree (`Struct`, `PlainStruct`, `ConverterDef`, `FieldConflicts`, `AbstractStruct`, `ConstAbstractStruct`) — these are pure data classes with no `Typespace` dependency (only depend on `common.*` + `il.ast.typed.Super`); they are consumed by the live new-typer Scala / TypeScript / C# Domain renderers (`DomainScalaStruct`, `DomainTSStruct`, `DomainCSStruct`, `DomainCompositeStructure`, `ScalaStruct`, `ScalaTypeConverter`, `DomainCSClass`) as the renderer-internal flat-struct vocabulary. **Final audit** `grep -rn "Typespace\b|IDLTyper\b|TypespaceImpl\b" idealingua-v1/`: returns only scaladoc / inline-comment historical references (legitimate pointers describing legacy / cross-reference rationale) + imports of the surviving `typespace.structures.*` data classes (`Struct`, `ConverterDef`, `PlainStruct`, `ConstAbstractStruct` — 4 importing files). No `import izumi.idealingua.model.typespace.Typespace`, no `: Typespace` parameter, no `new TypespaceImpl(...)` / `new IDLTyper(...)` / `new IDLPostTyper(...)` survives anywhere in live code. **Diff stat**: ~80 files changed; deleted ~50 files / ~3700 LOC (typespace tree + verification subtree + renderer tree + IDLTyper + DomainDefinition + LoaderTest); created 2 files (`IDLPretyper.scala`, `DomainMetadata.scala`) totaling ~70 LOC; rewrote 7 files (`LoadedDomain.scala`, `ModelResolver.scala`, `DuplicateDomainsRule.scala`, `NegativeSpec.scala`, `TranslatorDescriptor.scala`, `IDLError.scala`); edited ~9 consumer files (`CommandlineIDLCompiler`, `HarnessCorpus`, `IDLTestTools`, `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec`, `TyperDiagnosticsSpec`, `CSharp/Scala/TS TranslatorDescriptor`). **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (no regeneration needed; byte-equal across all three language corpora), `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42 (NegativeSpec's 9 `.must-reject` cases continue to reject — now via the new typer path; `TyperDiagnosticsSpec`'s 28-domain corpus continues to accept). **IMPL-11 fully collapsed**: no separate IMPL-11 scope remains; everything IMPL-11 would have delivered (the final atomic deletion of `Typespace` + `IDLTyper` + verification subtree + `LoadedDomain.Success.typespace`) was completed under IMPL-10d. The legacy typer + typespace machinery is gone end-to-end; the new typer pipeline (`NewTyperPipeline.run` invoked at translate-time by the three `Domain<Lang>Translator`s + `NegativeSpec` + `TyperDiagnosticsSpec`) is the sole typing path.

- **PR-02 IMPL-10c: delete legacy translator trees + TyperImpl enum** (2026-05-12) — Atomic deletion of the legacy translator tree across all three target languages plus the `TyperImpl` enum and the `--typer` CLI flag. **Deleted (37 files, ~6900 LOC)**: legacy translator entry points (`ScalaTranslator.scala`, `STContext.scala`, `AdtRenderer.scala`, `CompositeRenderer.scala`, `EnumRenderer.scala`, `IdRenderer.scala`, `InterfaceRenderer.scala`, `ServiceRenderer.scala`; `TypeScriptTranslator.scala`, `TSTContext.scala`, `TypeScriptImports.scala`, `TypeScriptTranslationTools.scala`; `CSharpTranslator.scala`, `CSTContext.scala`, `CSharpTranslationTools.scala`), legacy extension dispatchers + concrete extensions (`Scala*Extensions.scala`, `AnyvalExtension`, `CastDownExpandExtension`, `CastSimilarExtension`, `CastUpExtension`, `CirceTranslatorExtensionBase`; `TypeScript*Extensions`, `EnumHelpersExtension`, `IntrospectionExtension`; `CSharp*Extensions`, `JsonNetExtension`, `NUnitExtension`), legacy types families that the Domain renderers had already stopped consuming (`toscala/types/CompositeStructure.scala`, `FullServiceContext.scala`, `ServiceContext.scala`, `ServiceMethodProduct.scala`; `totypescript/types/TypeScriptTypeConverter.scala`; `tocsharp/types/CSharpClass.scala`, `CSharpField.scala`, `CSharpType.scala`), legacy `toscala/tools/ScalaTranslationTools.scala`, and `TyperImpl.scala`. **defaultExtensions migration**: extracted to `domain/<Lang>DefaultExtensions.scala` (`Seq.empty` — the Domain renderers invoke each `Domain<Ext>` helper directly at the appropriate call site rather than iterating a `Seq[<Lang>TranslatorExtension]`, so the legacy seq plays no runtime role on the new path). The three descriptors point at the new objects; consumers (`HarnessOptions.optionsFor`, `CommandlineIDLCompiler.getExt`, `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec`) round-trip the empty seq through `UntypedCompilerOptions.extensions` unchanged. **Marker traits preserved**: `ScalaTranslatorExtension` / `TypeScriptTranslatorExtension` / `CSharpTranslatorExtension` reduced to empty markers (`extends TranslatorExtension`); they keep `CompilerOptions[<Lang>TranslatorExtension, <Lang>BuildManifest]` and the existing test imports compiling without churn until `CompilerOptions[E, M]` collapses to an extension-less single-parameter form. **TyperImpl + `--typer` fate**: `TyperImpl` enum file deleted; `case TyperImpl.Legacy =>` branch retired from `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.compile`; `UntypedCompilerOptions.typerImpl` field dropped; `IDLCArgs.typerImpl` + the `--typer / -T` CLI flag (`P.typerImpl`) removed; `HarnessOptions.optionsFor(lang, typer)` collapses to `optionsFor(lang)`; `GoldenCompile.compileAll`'s per-language typer switch removed (NewTyper everywhere); `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec` collapses to a per-language smoke test against the new pipeline. **Descriptor surface**: `TranslatorDescriptor.make(typespace, options)` removed from trait + every concrete descriptor (the new path's `makeDomain(domain, parsed, options)` is the sole entry point). **Inlined**: legacy `CSharpField.safeName` (reserved-keyword guard) inlined verbatim into `DomainCSField` so the only legacy coupling on the C# side is removed; the legacy `CSharpImports.apply(definition, ...)(ts)` Typespace-driven factory methods deleted from the `CSharpImports` companion (the Domain path uses `DomainCSImports` directly and only consumes the `CSharpImports(imports: List[CSharpImport])` constructor); a minimal `TypeScriptImport` case class extracted to its own file so `DomainTSImports` keeps its import-record vocabulary unchanged. **Files NOT deleted** (kept by deliberate choice): layouters (`ScalaLayouter`, `TypescriptLayouter`, `CSharpLayouter` — they consume `Translated`, not Typespace; updated pre-IMPL-7a.1 to use `domainId`/`meta`); `toscala/products/`, `totypescript/products/`, `tocsharp/products/` (the Domain renderers all build `CogenProduct` / `RenderableCogenProduct` instances directly); `toscala/tools/ModuleTools.scala` + `ScalaMetaTools.scala` and the TS / CS `tools/ModuleTools.scala` (every Domain translator consumes them); `toscala/types/ClassSource.scala`, `ScalaField.scala`, `ScalaStruct.scala`, `ScalaType.scala`, `ScalaTypeConverter.scala`, `ScalaTypeImpl.scala`, `StructContext.scala`, `runtime/IDLRuntimeTypes` (consumed by the Domain Scala renderers + extensions); `tocsharp/CSharpImports.scala` (case class consumed by every C# Domain renderer); `tocsharp/CSharpImports`'s legacy companion factory methods stripped but the case class + render methods retained. `ClassSource.CsMethodInput` / `CsMethodOutput` collapsed from case classes carrying legacy payloads to case objects (DomainCompositeRenderer only branched on the tag, never read the fields). Typespace / IDLTyper / TypespaceImpl / `LoadedDomain.Success.typespace` / `typespace/structures/` / `typespace/verification/` deletions are deferred to **IMPL-10d** per scope. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (no regeneration needed; byte-equal across all three language corpora), `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. **Diff stat**: 59 files changed, +289 / −6931. **IMPL-10d scope**: `case TyperImpl.Legacy =>` is already gone, so IMPL-10d's remaining surface narrows to deleting `Typespace` trait + `TypespaceImpl` + `StructuralQueriesImpl` + `InheritanceQueriesImpl` + `TypeCollection` + `TypespaceVerifier` + `IDLTyper` + `IDLPostTyper` + `LoadedDomain.Success.typespace` field + the `typespace/structures/` subtree (PlainStruct / Struct / Inheritance / IndexedStruct / IndexedStructImpl) + the `typespace/verification/` subtree (`TypespaceVerifier` and its rule machinery); rewriting `ModelResolver.runVerifier` to no-op or wrap `NewTyperPipeline.run` diagnostics; rewriting `DuplicateDomainsRule` to read `parsed.id`; rewriting `LoadedDomain.Success` to carry only `parsed: DomainMeshResolved` + diagnostics. Once the `Typespace` parameter is gone, the legacy verification rule machinery currently surfaced through `TranslatorDescriptor.rules` may also collapse to the new-typer's structural diagnostics.

- **PR-02 IMPL-10b-fix: TS cross-domain mixin serialization (D1.toM2Serialized)** (2026-05-13) — Closes the wire-format-breaking defect surfaced by the IMPL-10b stop-condition: with `GoldenCompile` putting TypeScript on `TyperImpl.NewTyper`, `idltest.aliases#D1.toM2Serialized()` rendered an empty body — dropping the `f2` field contributed by the cross-domain `idltest.aliases2#M2` mixin. Root cause: `StructuralFlattener` (Fj harvest path, commit `cac39ba`) materialises foreign mixin fields into the consumer DTO's local `FlatStruct` so D1's flat already contains `f2`, but the foreign mixin's *own* `FlatStruct` was deliberately omitted from `Domain.flattenedStructs` (kept local-only via `localStructIds` filter so cast-similar peer scan + anyval candidate detection in the Scala renderers do not surface foreign types). The TS `DomainTSCompositeRenderer.renderDtoInterfaceSerializer` calls `DomainTSStruct.structureOf(iid)` to fetch the parent interface's slice for the `to<Iface>Serialized` body; for cross-domain `M2` the local-only `flattenedStructs` lookup missed and returned an empty `FlatStruct`. The legacy `Typespace.structure.structure(iid)` path transitively resolved foreign meshes via `ts.resolver.get(iid)` — the new IR's renderer-level access had no equivalent. Fix: surface foreign harvested flat structs through a new `Domain.crossDomainFlattenedStructs: Map[StructureId, FlatStruct]` field (defaults to empty for back-compat). `StructuralFlattener` BFS still flattens local-only into `flatBuf` for parity with legacy `Typespace.structures`, AND flattens foreign harvested views into a separate `foreignFlatBuf` (foreign diagnostics drop into a sink — the foreign domain owns its own typer pass + diagnostics). `Assembler` propagates the field. `DomainTSStruct.structureOf` consults `crossDomainFlattenedStructs` as a fallback when the primary lookup misses. **Why a separate map and not extending `flattenedStructs`**: `DomainCastSimilarExtension` (Scala) iterates `Domain.flattenedStructs.toList.collect` to detect same-signature peer DTOs; `DomainAnyvalExtension.canBeAnyValField` consults `flattenedStructs.get(i)` for arbitrary interface ids including cross-domain. Adding foreign harvested entries to `flattenedStructs` would change both predicates' results for cross-domain interface references (the iterators would surface foreign types in language-specific contexts that legacy never did — peer-cast discovery and AnyVal-eligibility scoring would silently re-key off cross-domain shape data). A dedicated cross-domain map preserves the local-only semantics of `flattenedStructs` while making the foreign data available to consumers that explicitly opt in. **`GoldenCompile` flip**: TypeScript moved off `TyperImpl.Legacy` onto `TyperImpl.NewTyper` — all three languages now share the new-typer path. **Goldens regenerated**: 17 TS modules updated for the introspector field-order + module-export-order shapes that came along with the new typer's BFS-derived field order (already documented as cosmetic in the IMPL-10b stop-condition note). `idltest/clones/index.ts` gained `export * from './M1';` (legacy index missed the M1 clone — corrected by the new emitter). **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok against regenerated goldens, `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42. Files edited: `Domain.scala` (+ field with default `Map.empty`), `ResolvedDomain.scala` (+ field with default `Map.empty`), `Assembler.scala` (propagate field), `StructuralFlattener.scala` (compute foreign flats into separate buf with separate diag sink), `DomainTSStruct.scala` (`structureOf` fallback to `crossDomainFlattenedStructs`), `GoldenCompile.scala` (TS dispatch → `NewTyper` + doc rewrite). 17 TS goldens regenerated (`anyvals/Test00Data2AnyVal.ts`, `anyvals/Test01DataAnyVal1.ts`, `anyvals/Test01DataAnyVal2.ts`, `clones/index.ts`, `datainheritance/TestData2.ts`, `datainheritancetransitive/Coupon.ts`, `datainheritancetransitive/MassCoupon.ts`, `diamonds/DTO1.ts`, `dtofields/Point.ts`, `phase/Name_view.ts`, `substraction/PublicUser2.ts`, `substraction/User2.ts`, `upcasts/Item.ts`, `domain01/AnotherTestObject.ts`, `domain01/GenericFailure.ts`, `domain01/PrivateTestObject.ts`, `domain02/DTO1.ts`). **Path to IMPL-10c**: the IMPL-10b stop-condition is closed; the `case TyperImpl.Typescript => Legacy` branch in `GoldenCompile.compileAll` is retired. IMPL-10c can proceed to eliminate `XxxTranslator.defaultExtensions` companion-object dependency + delete the legacy `XxxTranslator` / `XxxTContext` / `extensions/` / `types/` converter families per the IMPL-10 decomposition.

- **PR-02 IMPL-10b: retire byte-parity test apparatus (port-time oracle, superseded by FROZEN contracts)** (2026-05-12) — Deletes the 19 (actually 23) byte-parity spec files that used `IDLTyper` / `TypespaceImpl` / mirrored-`protected`-legacy-render bodies as the parity ORACLE during the IMPL-7a.2 Phase B / IMPL-7b.2 / IMPL-7c.2 ports. Specs deleted under `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/test/scala/izumi/idealingua/harness/`: `DomainAliasRendererSpec`, `DomainEnumRendererSpec`, `DomainTSAliasRendererSpec`, `DomainTSEnumRendererSpec`, `DomainTSIdRendererSpec`, `DomainTSCompositeRendererSpec`, `DomainTSInterfaceRendererSpec`, `DomainTSAdtRendererSpec`, `DomainTSServiceRendererSpec`, `DomainTSImportsSpec`, `DomainTSIntrospectionExtensionSpec`, `DomainTSEnumHelpersExtensionSpec`, `DomainCSAliasRendererSpec`, `DomainCSEnumRendererSpec`, `DomainCSIdRendererSpec`, `DomainCSCompositeRendererSpec`, `DomainCSInterfaceRendererSpec`, `DomainCSAdtRendererSpec`, `DomainCSServiceRendererSpec`, `DomainCSImportsSpec`, `DomainCSJsonNetExtensionSpec`, `ScalaTranslatorByteParitySpec`, `ScalaTyperParitySpec` — 23 files total. The retired apparatus served its purpose: every TS / C# / Scala renderer was driven to byte-parity against the legacy renderer during port; once production-path swap landed (Scala IMPL-7a.2 M6 / TS IMPL-7b.2 M5 / C# IMPL-7c.2 M5) the four FROZEN harness contracts (`verifyGoldens` + `runWireFixtures` + `runCrossLangInterop` + `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test`) became the going-forward regression guard. **Production-side simplification**: `RegenerateNewTyperMain` + the `regenerateGoldensNewTyper` sbt task removed (redundant since IMPL-9 flipped the canonical default to `TyperImpl.NewTyper`); `GoldenGenerator.regenerate`'s public `scalaTyper` parameter removed; per-language typer dispatch internalized in `GoldenCompile.compileAll`. `TyperDiagnosticsSpec` + `NegativeSpec` + the structural-renderer specs that do not consult the legacy oracle (`DomainAdtRendererSpec`, `DomainCompositeRendererSpec`, `DomainIdRendererSpec`, `DomainInterfaceRendererSpec`, `DomainServiceRendererSpec`, `DomainAnyvalExtensionSpec`, `DomainCast{Down,Similar,Up}{Expand,}ExtensionSpec`, `DomainCirceExtensionSpec`) stay. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (no regeneration needed; byte-equal), `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 42/42 (dropped from 99 — the retired oracle suite accounted for 57 tests). **Stop-condition / latent defect surfaced**: with `GoldenCompile` initially flipped to put TypeScript on `TyperImpl.NewTyper` (the brief's literal direction), `verifyGoldens` flagged 18 TS modules diverging — including a substantive defect at `golden/typescript/idltest/aliases/D1.ts` where the new typer's TS path emits an empty `toM2Serialized` body that DROPS the cross-domain `f2` field contributed by `idltest.aliases2#M2` (wire-format-breaking — `D1.toM2Serialized()` would serialize as `{}` instead of `{"f2": "..."}`). 17 other TS divergences are introspector field-order / module-export-order cosmetic changes. The brief's premise "all three translator backends fully ported" did NOT hold for TS at HEAD: `IMPL-7b.2 M5` (commit `b07ae6b`, 2026-05-12) stated "TS goldens did NOT need regeneration" at swap time, but one or more of the four post-M5 commits (`91d78c0` DomainTSImports/DomainCSImports shims, `545625b` DomainTypespaceFacade removal, `6321405` DomainTSTypeConverter port, `99448bc` orphan-compat deletion) regressed the cross-domain mixin flattening in TS — the regression was hidden by `GoldenCompile`'s `case _ => TyperImpl.Legacy` dispatch. Resolution for IMPL-10b: keep `GoldenCompile`'s TS dispatch on `TyperImpl.Legacy`, do NOT commit regenerated TS goldens, document the residual `TyperImpl.Legacy` reference + the cross-domain-mixin defect as a forward gate; Scala + C# continue on `TyperImpl.NewTyper`. **Also fixed**: latent defect in `GoldenGenerator.deleteRecursively` — `Files.walk` enumerates `golden/typescript/irt` (a checked-in symlink into the TS runtime-rpc resources tree) as a leaf entry and `Files.delete` then unlinks the symlink. Added `Files.isSymbolicLink` skip + root-self skip so subsequent regen runs are idempotent across the corpus + preserved the symlink artifact. Empirically reproduced (1st regen wiped the symlink; 2nd regen restored from git survives untouched). **Files deleted**: 23 specs + 1 entry point (`RegenerateNewTyperMain` in `HarnessMain.scala`) + 1 sbt task (`regenerateGoldensNewTyper` in `build.sbt`). **Files edited**: `GoldenCompile.scala` (drop `scalaTyper` parameter; internalize per-language dispatch; document the TS-Legacy pin), `GoldenGenerator.scala` (drop `scalaTyper` parameter; fix symlink-preservation defect), `HarnessOptions.scala` (drop `optionsFor(lang)` doc churn), `HarnessMain.scala` (delete `RegenerateNewTyperMain` object), `build.sbt` (delete `regenerateGoldensNewTyper` taskKey + body). **Path to IMPL-10c**: diagnose the TS NewTyper cross-domain-mixin defect at `D1.toM2Serialized`; once `verifyGoldens` with `GoldenCompile` putting TS on `TyperImpl.NewTyper` is byte-clean, flip the dispatch + retire the `case Typescript => Legacy` branch + delete the legacy `XxxTranslator` / `XxxTContext` / `extensions` / `types` converter families per the IMPL-10c decomposition in the IMPL-10 entry below.

- **PR-02 IMPL-10: scoped deletion of orphaned `compat/` shims** (2026-05-12) — Atomic deletion of `idealingua-v1/idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/compat/DomainAsTypespace.scala` (37 LOC) and `idealingua-v1/idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/compat/DomainTypespaceFacade.scala` (68 LOC). Pre-deletion audit `grep -rn "DomainAsTypespace\|DomainTypespaceFacade" idealingua-v1/` returned only scaladoc / inline-comment hits — no `import`, no constructor call, no field type. The IMPL-10-prep-Cs2 commit (`5b922fe`) had already eliminated the last code-level use of both files. Updated the stale scaladoc reference in `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.scala` to record the scoped deletion and surface the deferred work (the `case TyperImpl.Legacy =>` branch + `IDLTyper` / `TypespaceImpl` / `Typespace` tree). **STOP CONDITION on the original "full atomic deletion" spec**: the full scope ("delete legacy typer + Typespace query machinery + TyperImpl enum + LoadedDomain.Success.typespace + ScalaTranslator/TypeScriptTranslator/CSharpTranslator + their TContext / extensions / types subtrees") cannot land atomically — 19 byte-parity spec files in `idealingua-v1-test-harness/src/test/` construct `new TypespaceImpl(legacyDomain)` and call `new IDLTyper(parsed).perform()` against the live legacy code path as the byte-parity ORACLE; `ScalaTranslatorByteParitySpec` and `GoldenCompile` exercise `TyperImpl.Legacy` directly; `LoaderTest`, `DuplicateDomainsRule`, `ModelResolver.runVerifier`, `LoadedDomain.Success.typespace`, every `Xxx{Translator,TContext,TranslatorExtension,TranslatorExtensions}` and the `tocsharp/types` / `totypescript/types` converter families (~80 files) are alive on the legacy branch and have no Domain-only replacement; `Domain*Translator.defaultExtensions` (the new-path companion) is sourced from `XxxTranslator.defaultExtensions` on the legacy companions. Removing the legacy typer in one commit would orphan ~100 files and destroy the cross-typer parity oracle simultaneously. The full deletion is decomposed in the IMPL-10/11 row into **IMPL-10b** (migrate 19 byte-parity specs from legacy-oracle comparison to golden-file comparison), **IMPL-10c** (eliminate `XxxTranslator.defaultExtensions` companion-object dependency + delete legacy `XxxTranslator` + `XxxTContext` + `extensions/` + `types/` converter families), **IMPL-10d** (delete `TyperImpl.Legacy` + `IDLTyper` + `Typespace` tree + `verification/` rules + `LoadedDomain.Success.typespace` + descriptor `make()` legacy overload + `--typer` CLI flag + `UntypedCompilerOptions.typerImpl`; rewrite `ModelResolver.runVerifier` and `DuplicateDomainsRule`). **Files deleted**: 2 (`DomainAsTypespace.scala`, `DomainTypespaceFacade.scala`). **Net LOC delta**: −105 production LOC (37 + 68), +1 comment-only edit in `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade.scala`. **Verification matrix (4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (no regeneration needed; byte-equal), `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 99/99. The two deletions are pure dead-code removal — the production paths for Scala/TS/CSharp under `TyperImpl.NewTyper` already stopped consuming both files pre-Cs2, so no behaviour change is possible from this commit.

- **PR-02 IMPL-7b Phase B M2: TS identifier + DTO + interface renderers (unit-tested)** (2026-05-12) — Structural renderer port for the Domain-consuming TypeScript translator. Mirrors the Scala Phase B M3 pattern (commit `77d6c84`): `DomainTSStruct` adapter + three pre-extension renderers (`DomainTSIdRenderer`, `DomainTSCompositeRenderer`, `DomainTSInterfaceRenderer`) under `idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/totypescript/domain/`. `DomainTSStruct.fromFlat(id, flat, supers, domain): izumi.idealingua.model.typespace.structures.Struct` mirrors `DomainScalaStruct.fromFlat` 1:1 — the legacy `StructuralQueriesImpl.scala:41` sort key (`(distance, definedBy.toString, -definedWithIndex).reverse`) and covariant duplicate-field dedup are language-agnostic and encode what `typespace.structure.structure(...)` returns regardless of the consumer. `DomainTSStruct.implId(iid) = DTOId(iid, "Struct")` matches legacy `typespace.tools.implId`. Each structural renderer takes a `Typespace` parameter per-call (kept threaded from Phase A so `TypeScriptTypeConverter.toNativeType` and `TypeScriptImports.apply` can be reused unchanged); the renderer looks up the legacy `TypeDef` via `ts.domain.types.find(_.id == newIR.id)` for `TypeScriptImports.apply`. Identifier widens `IdField → Field` directly (no flattening — identifiers have no inheritance). DTO and Interface project from `Domain.flattenedStructs(id)` via `DomainTSStruct.fromFlat`. Extension chain omitted (default TS extensions have no `handleIdentifier`/`handleDTO`/`handleInterface` handler — pre-extension product is byte-equal to post-extension). **Production path unchanged**: `DomainTypeScriptTranslator.translate()` still goes through Phase A delegation; M2 renderers are wired only at the unit-test boundary. **LOC**: production 119 (`DomainTSStruct`) + 143 (`DomainTSIdRenderer`) + 179 (`DomainTSCompositeRenderer`) + 183 (`DomainTSInterfaceRenderer`) = 624; specs 218 + 279 + 294 = 791; total 1415 (under 1500 budget). **6 new byte-parity unit tests** across `DomainTSIdRendererSpec` (single-primitive, multi-primitive sort-stable), `DomainTSCompositeRendererSpec` (single-field DTO, multi-field declaration-order), `DomainTSInterfaceRendererSpec` (single-field iface, multi-field declaration-order); each spec mirrors the legacy `protected` rendering body inline at the test boundary and asserts pre-extension byte-equality on every `Product` field (`identifier`/`identifierInterface`/`iface`/`companion`/`more` + `header` + `preamble`). **Verification matrix**: 4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green (2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each: `verifyGoldens` ok, `runWireFixtures` 25/19/20, `runCrossLangInterop` 112/10/6, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 59/59 — the 6 new TS byte-parity cases add to the 53 prior tests). **Touch-point resolution from M1 hand-off**: (#1) impl-DTO companion name via `DomainTSStruct.implId(iid)` (structural equivalent to legacy `typespace.tools.implId`, does not require `EphemeralOrigin.InterfaceMirror` projection at M2 — the synthesized DTO id is identical by construction). (#2) DTO field flattening via `Domain.flattenedStructs` per the Scala M3 baseline. (#3) `TypeScriptImports.apply(ts, ...)` keeps legacy `Typespace` threading at M2 — a `DomainTSImports` shim is deferred to M6 production-swap. (#4) Identifier's `parentsInherited(i.id)` equivalent is `Domain.parents` but ordering is not yet verified, so we keep legacy `ts.inheritance.parentsInherited` threading. (#5) `TypeScriptTypeConverter.toNativeType(ts, ...)` keeps legacy `Typespace` per the M1 recommendation. **No IR gaps surfaced** at M2. **M3 next**: TS ADT renderer + service / buzzer family + `ServiceMethodProduct`-equivalent helpers (`renderRPCMethodSignature` / `renderRPCClientMethod` / `renderServiceDispatcher` / `renderServiceServer` / `renderBuzzerDispatcher`).

- **PR-02 IMPL-7b Phase B M1: `DomainTSAliasRenderer` + `DomainTSEnumRenderer` (unit-tested)** (2026-05-12) — First concrete renderer port for the Domain-consuming TypeScript translator. Mirrors the Scala Phase B M1 pattern (commit `0a89850`): a minimal `DomainTSContext` (`domain: Domain` + `parsed: DomainMeshResolved` + `options: TypescriptTranslatorOptions` + `conv: TypeScriptTypeConverter` + `modules: ModuleTools` + `manifest`) and two pre-extension renderers under `idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/totypescript/domain/`. `DomainTSAliasRenderer.renderAlias(i, ts)` returns an `AliasProduct(content)` byte-identical to legacy `TypeScriptTranslator.renderAlias`'s fixed comment block (TypeScript does not natively support type aliases with constructor / casting semantics, so the legacy renderer just emits a `// export type X = Y;` reference comment); the `Typespace` parameter is threaded per-call because `TypeScriptTypeConverter.toNativeType` takes one — under the new typer's full dealiasing pass `i.target` is never `AliasId`, so the converter's `ts(al)` paths are not exercised. `DomainTSEnumRenderer.renderEnumeration(i)` returns an `EnumProduct(content, preamble)` byte-identical to legacy. Extension chain is omitted at M1: default TS extension set is `EnumHelpersExtension` + `IntrospectionExtension`; neither handles aliases, and the enum handler depends on legacy `TSTContext` + `Enumeration` — M2+ scope. **Production path unchanged**: `DomainTypeScriptTranslator.translate()` still goes through Phase A delegation (re-derives a legacy `Typespace` via `IDLTyper(parsed).perform()` and calls the legacy `TypeScriptTranslator`); the new renderers are not yet wired into emission. **LOC** (under the 600 budget): production 36 (`DomainTSContext`) + 53 (`DomainTSAliasRenderer`) + 43 (`DomainTSEnumRenderer`) = 132; specs 153 (`DomainTSAliasRendererSpec`, 2 cases: alias→primitive, alias→enum) + 133 (`DomainTSEnumRendererSpec`, 2 cases: mono, RGB order-preserving) = 286; total 418. **Verification matrix**: 4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8 lines all green (Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each: `verifyGoldens` ok, `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 53/53 — adds the 4 new TS byte-parity cases to the 49 prior tests, matching the Scala M1 wrap-up). The legacy `protected` `renderAlias` / `renderEnumeration` methods are mirrored at the test boundary (by-inspection citations to `TypeScriptTranslator.scala:233-258` and `:450-464`). **No IR gaps surfaced for TS-M1**: alias rendering reads only `i.id.name` + `i.target` (no `Domain` lookup beyond what the converter does); enum rendering reads only `i.id.name` + `i.members.map(_.value)`. **M2 next**: TS identifier + DTO + interface renderers (the structural family). Anticipated IR-touch points: (i) the legacy interface impl-DTO companion name is `${iface.name}${typespace.tools.implId(iface).name}` — typically `<Iface>Struct` via the synthesized `EphemeralOrigin.InterfaceMirror` DTO; need to project this from `Domain.members` / `ephemeralsOf` rather than `typespace.tools.implId`. (ii) DTO serialization reads `typespace.structure.structure(i).all` (recursively flattened inherited fields) — covered by `Domain.flattenedStructs` per the Scala M3 baseline. (iii) `TypeScriptImports.apply(ts, ...)` walks `typespace`-shaped lookup; either keep the legacy `Typespace` threading at M2 (simplest) or land a `DomainTSImports` shim. (iv) Identifier's `parentsInherited(i.id)` is `Domain.parents`-equivalent. No new typer fixes anticipated.

- **PR-02 IMPL-9: flip `--typer` default from `Legacy` to `NewTyper`** (2026-05-12) — Closes the IMPL-9 gating row. Landed as three commits: (1) `PR-02 IMPL-9-prep: regenerate Scala goldens for new-typer default` — regenerates 73 Scala goldens under `idealingua-v1-test-defs/golden/scala/` via the new typer, flips the harness defaults that previously pinned `TyperImpl.Legacy` (`GoldenCompile.compileAll`, `GoldenGenerator.regenerate`, `HarnessOptions.optionsFor(lang)` single-arg overload), updates `NewTyperFeatureFlagSpec` to assert the new default, and fixes 3 pre-existing failing `EphemeralSynthesizerSpec` cases (the spec was lagging behind the post-Fi1 `EphemeralSynthesizer.synthesizeAltBranch` Singular-Builtin pass-through semantics — wrapper-DTO assertions were rewritten to assert `AdtMember.typeId` flow-through). (2) `PR-02 IMPL-9: flip --typer default from Legacy to NewTyper` — 2-line flip in `UntypedCompilerOptions.typerImpl` + `IDLCArgs.typerImpl` + `IDLCArgs.parse` getOrElse default. (3) This tasks.md update. **Audit at flip-time** (`ScalaTranslatorByteParitySpec`): `SubstantiveBaseline = 23` byte-divergent modules, `AcceptedDivergences.size = 44` ordering-residual modules — none of these break the wire-format invariant. The IMPL-9 STOP-report ledger from prior cycles (167 → 113 → 110 → 80 → 79 → … → 23 across Fa/Fb/Fc/Fd/Fe/Ff/Fg/Fh/Fi/Fj) is the substantive history; the residual 23 are accepted-divergence categories (F-clone-newtype, F-subtraction, F-cross-domain-anyval, F-composite-mixin, F-implementing-dtos-missing, F-alias-mixin-scope, F-adt-companion-shape) documented in the spec's `SubstantiveDivergences` comment. **TS / C# unchanged**: both languages stay on `TyperImpl.Legacy` via Phase A delegation (`DomainTypeScriptTranslator` / `DomainCSharpTranslator` re-derive a legacy `Typespace` from `parsed`), observe the new default transparently, and their goldens are byte-stable across the flip — confirmed by `git status` showing zero modifications under `golden/typescript/` and `golden/csharp/`. The `DomainAsTypespace` adapter remains in tree until IMPL-10/11. **Full verification matrix on top of the flip (8 contract lines)**: Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each — `verifyGoldens` ok (all goldens match), `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 49/49. Plus `idealingua-v1-modelJVM/test` 84/84 on both Scala versions (the 3 prior `EphemeralSynthesizerSpec` failures are now green). **IMPL-10 / IMPL-11 gating** updated in the Milestone 2 row: the flip half of IMPL-9..N is closed; legacy-typer deletion remains gated on Phase B ports for both TS+C# (currently Phase A delegation re-derives a legacy `Typespace`).

- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fj: cross-domain mixin flattening in StructuralFlattener** (2026-05-12) — Closes the final residual compile-gate defect from Fi. **Defect**: `idltest/aliases/D1.scala` failed to compile because `f2` was dropped from the case class — `data D1 { & A1; & A2 }` where `A2 = idltest.aliases2#M2` references a mixin in a foreign domain, and Phase 6 `StructuralFlattener` was per-domain (`views.get(idltest.aliases2#M2) = None`), so foreign-mixin fields never reached the local `FlatStruct`. **Fix (Option a — recursive cross-domain resolution)**: `StructuralFlattener.apply` gains a second overload `apply(rd, family)` and `NewTyperPipeline` calls it with the Phase 0 `FamilyIndex`. For each direct supertype whose `path.domain != rd.id`, the flattener runs `ScopeBuilder` + `NameResolver` on the foreign mesh (cached per-domain via `family.domains(d)`), harvests its `Dto`/`Interface` structs into `views`/`directSupers`/`directInterfaces`/`directConcepts`, and recurses over foreign-of-foreign supertypes. Foreign-domain diagnostics are dropped — the foreign domain runs its own pipeline pass and surfaces them through its own result, so re-emitting here would only double-report. Foreign harvested views participate in BFS as ancestors but do NOT receive their own `FlatStruct` entry in the local domain (they belong to a different domain's `flattenedStructs` map). The single-arg `apply(rd)` overload is preserved for tests; production cross-domain support flows through `apply(rd, family)`. **Files**: `idealingua-v1-model/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/typer/phase/StructuralFlattener.scala` (+overload + foreign-harvest block + local-only flatten gate); `idealingua-v1-transpilers/src/main/scala/izumi/idealingua/translator/compat/NewTyperPipeline.scala` (call site passes `family`). **Compile-gate result**: `regenerateGoldensNewTyper` + `idealingua-v1-test-harness/Compile/compile`, errors drop from **1 → 0** on both Scala 2.13.18 and 3.8.3. **SubstantiveBaseline 23 → 23** (unchanged — the D1 module remains in `AcceptedDivergences` because the cross-domain cast `D1_upcast_M2` is still not emitted; that is a separate renderer-side concern, not a flattener gap). Verification matrix: 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each: compile gate 0 errors; `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 49/49 green; `ScalaTranslatorByteParitySpec` baseline=23 holds; `idealingua-v1-modelJVM/test` 81/84 (3 pre-existing `EphemeralSynthesizerSpec` failures unchanged by this commit). **IMPL-9 is now feasible**: with 0 compile-gate errors on both Scala versions, `TyperImpl.NewTyper` can be flipped as the default in `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade` against `SubstantiveBaseline = 23` accepted structural divergences (none of which break wire format).

- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fi: compile gate + F-alt-output-cast-targets-nonexistent + F-covariant-field-type-narrowing** (2026-05-12) — Adds an opt-in **compile gate** for the new-typer Scala output and closes two real type-error defect classes that bytewise-equality (`ScalaTranslatorByteParitySpec`) missed because it never type-checked. **Compile gate**: `GoldenCompile.compileAll` accepts a `scalaTyper: TyperImpl` parameter (default `Legacy`); new sbt task `regenerateGoldensNewTyper` invokes `RegenerateNewTyperMain` which passes `TyperImpl.NewTyper`. After regeneration the harness's standard `Compile/compile` transitively type-checks every emitted module against the IRT runtime (the `golden/scala` tree is already on `Compile / unmanagedSourceDirectories`), surfacing any new-typer codegen type error that survives parse. Default `regenerateGoldens` / `verifyGoldens` still produce legacy output so the 4 FROZEN harness contracts stay green trivially. **Commits**: `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2: add compile gate for new-typer output` (build.sbt + HarnessOptions + HarnessMain + GoldenGenerator + GoldenCompile); `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fi1: alt-output cast targets` (EphemeralSynthesizer drops the `Singular(Builtin)` wrapper DTO synthesis — legacy never created one, the ADT's `AdtMember.typeId` is the original Builtin; AdtMembersRule skips ephemeral ADTs to accept the resulting Builtin branches; DomainServiceMethodProduct.withAdtCirce wraps the service-output ADT with `<Name>Circe` trait + companion-base `extends` so service codec sites' `value.asJson` and `packet.as[Output]` resolve; DomainCastSimilarExtension.signatureOf renders Generic args recursively so `list[Package]` no longer collides with `list[str]`); `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fi2: covariant field-type narrowing in cast body` (DomainCastDownExpandExtension filters parent vs local fields by FULL Field equality instead of name-only, so when the implementor covariantly overrides a parent field with a narrower type the override stays in `localFields` and surfaces as a `using(field: <NarrowType>)` parameter — mirrors legacy `StructuralQueriesImpl.converters` line 197-214). **Gate result**: after `regenerateGoldensNewTyper` + `idealingua-v1-test-harness/Compile/compile`, errors drop from **~50** (pre-fix) to **1** — D1.scala (idltest.aliases / cross-domain `M2.f2` mixin field missing). The residual D1 defect is the cross-domain mixin flattening gap in Phase 6 (`StructuralFlattener` is per-domain and `views.get(idltest.aliases2#M2) = None`); fixing it requires plumbing the family-of-domains' resolved `FlatStruct`s through Phase 6, deferred per stop condition (new defect category). **SubstantiveBaseline 24 → 23** (Fi1 closed one byte-parity divergence as a side-effect of the alt-output wrapper removal). Verification matrix: 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 49/49 green; default `verifyGoldens` legacy mode unchanged. Path to IMPL-9: compile gate is now operational and one defect away from passing on all 28 domains — the next cycle should close cross-domain mixin flattening before flipping `TyperImpl.NewTyper` as the default in `TypespaceCompilerBaseFacade`.

- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fh: high-impact substantive cycle (F-subtraction + F-clone-newtype + F-implementing-dtos-missing)** (2026-05-12) — Closes three of the highest-impact F-followup families from the wrap-up audit. **SubstantiveBaseline 35 → 24** (–11 modules across three commits, plus two AcceptedDivergences entries cleared as a side-effect and four AST entries reclassified to accepted ordering). **Commits**: `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fh1` (F-subtraction — `StructuralFlattener` now expands `removedConcepts` recursively to flat field names during BFS, mirroring legacy `FieldExtractor.extractRemoved` + `filterFields`; 35 → 32, PublicUser1/PublicUser2/User2 close). `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fh2` (F-clone-newtype + parents-graph asymmetry — `clone X into Y { ... }` with non-empty modifiers materializes Y as the same kind as X via new helpers `ScopeBuilder.newtypeRegisteredId` / `findRawSource` and `NameResolver.fixNewType.mergeNewtype` (prepends source as `+ concept`); `StructuralFlattener.transitiveParents` rewritten to match legacy `safeAllParents` asymmetric concept-vs-interface recursion (`InheritanceQueriesImpl.scala:32-79`) so interface-via-interface chains stay interface-only and concept walks recurse through both edge types; 32 → 30, two ordering-accepted entries (`TypeInfo.scala`, `LengthInBytes.scala`) became byte-identical as a side-effect). `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fh3` (F-implementing-dtos-missing — `DomainCirceTranslatorExtensionBase.emitForInterface` now surfaces every descendant interface's mirror DTO (`DTOId(Sub, "Struct")`) as an encoder implementor, mirroring legacy `implementingDtos(I)` which keys on `parentsInherited` and naturally includes those mirrors via the `Sub.Struct extends Sub` synthesis; 30 → 24, NotiBase/NotiWithFile gain the case-arms and along with BoolNode/IntNode/IfNode/LamNode collapse to legacy-HashMap iteration-order residuals — moved to `AcceptedDivergences` category A). Verification matrix: 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 49/49 green. Path to IMPL-9: remaining 24 substantive divergences cluster around `F-composite-mixin` (PrivateMixin/PrivateMixinPrivateParent/TestInterface2/Identifiable `using(...)` parameter lists), `F-cross-domain-anyval` (ImportIdService.MixiInput AnyVal mixin), `F-alias-mixin-scope` (D1/M1.Struct), `F-deep-diamond` (domain02 DTO1), `F-adt-companion-shape` (TestAliasServ.TestADTIdReturnOutput), and a handful of cast-helper field-set differences (CovariantDTO2/InheritedCovariant/Name_stored/PrivateTestObject upcast targets, IA1/IA2 mirror cast targets, AppNode/FloatNode/SymNode `typeinfo: TypeInfo` vs `tpe: Type`, PersonalAttributes/SecurityAttributes `_downcast_extend_*` targets, M2.Struct field order, Name_view field type, M1 Struct cast target). None of these change wire format. **Recommendation**: IMPL-9 is feasible now if the user accepts the 24 remaining structural divergences as known-incompatibilities; otherwise the highest-impact remaining family is F-composite-mixin (5 modules).

- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fg: byte-parity spec wrap-up — split accepted-residual from substantive** (2026-05-12) — User-locked decision codified: HashMap / Set iteration order divergences in the legacy IR are accepted as cosmetic, because the wire format is preserved regardless (Encoder dispatches by runtime type; Decoder tries cases until one succeeds; named-arg constructor calls are order-independent at the call site; cast-helper emission order is purely cosmetic). The byte-parity spec is refactored to partition every observed divergence into `AcceptedDivergences` (44 entries — 28 driven by HashMap iteration: Circe encoder case-arm order, named-arg / apply param order, case-class declaration order from HashMap-backed accumulators; 16 driven by Set iteration in `compatibleDtos` / `StructuralQueriesImpl.converters` picking a different "first" peer for singleton `_cast_into_*` / `_downcast_extend_*` extensions) and `SubstantiveDivergences` (gated count `SubstantiveBaseline = 35` — was conflated `KnownDivergenceBaseline = 79`). Spec also fails on stale accepted entries so the gate cannot silently mask future regressions. **Substantive defect families at this baseline (each becomes its own F-followup):** F-clone-newtype (~7 modules: package-object missing `type M2 = M0`; `M2.scala` legacy-only; CovariantDTO2 / InheritedCovariant / Name_stored / Name / PrivateTestObject emit asymmetric upcasts from clone-derived structs); F-subtraction (6 modules — PublicUser1/2 retain subtracted fields, SecurityAttributes/User1/User2/PersonalAttributes downcast/upcast targets reflect missing `- field` semantics); F-cross-domain-anyval (1 module — ImportIdService.MixiInput missing `with AnyVal` because new IR's `flattenedStructs` is per-domain; requires either pre-computing every family-member Domain or threading FamilyIndex into the renderer — architectural change deferred); F-anyval-predicate-singlefield (PARTIALLY CLOSED in commit `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fg2`: dedupe `FlatStruct.fields` by field name in AnyVal + Circe predicates so re-declared same-typed parent fields like `IA2 { & IA1; Int: i32 }` collapse to size 1; the affected module IA2.scala still carries other substantive divergences so the count metric did not move); F-composite-mixin (5 modules — PrivateMixin / PrivateMixinPrivateParent / TestInterface2 (diamonds) / Identifiable (upcasts) / Name_view emit different `using(...)` parameter lists: flattened fields in new vs original composite mixin reference in legacy); F-implementing-dtos-missing (~10 modules — NotiBase / NotiWithFile / PrivateMixinParent / PersonalAttributes / 7 AST nodes / IA1 — encoder missing one or more `case v: X.Struct =>` arms because the implementing DTO is not registered in the new IR's parent set); F-alias-mixin-scope (2 modules — D1 has different field set due to alias-mixin scope; M1.Struct emits `_cast_into_D1` in new but `_upcast_Struct` in legacy); F-deep-diamond / cross-domain inheritance (1 module — domain02 DTO1 declares extra inherited fields); F-adt-companion-shape (1 module — TestAliasServ.TestADTIdReturnOutput companion legacy `trait XCirce` vs new `object X extends IDLAdt`). **Commits**: `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fg1` (spec refactor + AcceptedDivergences set of 44 ordering-residuals + SubstantiveBaseline=35), `PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fg2` (dedupe FlatStruct.fields by name in 3 AnyVal predicates + 1 Circe path; closes the IA2 AnyVal-vs-deriveEncoder partial divergence; baseline stays at 35 because IA2 still diverges for other substantive defects). Verification matrix: 4 FROZEN × 2 Scala = 8/8 green (Scala 2.13.18 + 3.8.3 each: `verifyGoldens` ok, `runWireFixtures` 25 Scala + 19 TypeScript + 20 CSharp, `runCrossLangInterop` 112 verified / 10 skipped / 6 excluded, `idealingua-v1-test-harness/test` 49/49). Path to IMPL-9: flipping `TyperImpl.NewTyper` as the default is gated on `SubstantiveBaseline == 0` on both Scala versions; 35 substantive divergences remain across ~9 F-followup families, none of which are blocking the wire-format invariant — they manifest as observable structural changes (missing case-arms, wrong cast targets, missing AnyVal mixin, subtraction semantics, clone-newtype shape, etc) but the affected goldens still serialise and deserialise correctly because the per-language wire-format harness (`runWireFixtures` + `runCrossLangInterop`) measures the wire layer, not source bytes. **Recommendation**: IMPL-9 may proceed against `SubstantiveBaseline > 0` if the user is willing to accept the listed structural divergences as known-incompatibilities documented per F-followup; otherwise close the F-clone-newtype + F-subtraction + F-implementing-dtos-missing families (the three with the highest module count) in subsequent cycles before flipping the default.

- **PR-02 IMPL-7a.2-Fd: Circe layout (defect #2)** (2026-05-12) — Closes the Circe-layout entry in the IMPL-9 F-followup catalog. **Defect #2 (interface-companion `StructCirce` trait + `Struct_upcast_*` set + AnyVal on impl Struct + cast-up field-order)** root cause: legacy `CompositeRenderer.defns(_, CsInterface)` rendered the interface impl DTO through the FULL extension chain — `AnyvalExtension.handleComposite` prepended `AnyVal` to the impl case class header when its single field qualified; `CirceTranslatorExtensionBase.handleComposite` synthesised the `<Iface>StructCirce` trait (derived codec or AnyVal `forProduct1` codec) and prepended its init to the inner `object Struct`; `CastUpExtension.handleComposite` emitted `Struct_upcast_Struct` (self) + `Struct_upcast_<Iface>` (parent) inside the inner companion. The new `DomainInterfaceRenderer.renderInterface` rendered the impl through `DomainCompositeRenderer.defns` only, without any extension chain, so none of those Defns ever appeared inside the interface companion.
